I just don't have the interest to do them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, lately?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I wouldn't really say I feel depressed or sad all day. It's more just... like I was saying, I just don't care about doing things anymore. But I don't really feel down in that way.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt down or in a depressed mood most of the day, nearly every day, lately?\n\nPatient: Like I just said, no, I don't think so. I really wouldn't call it feeling depressed or sad. I just don't have the drive to do much anymore, but my mood itself isn't really down like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you still enjoy things that used to make you happy, like dance class or trying new recipes, or do they just feel like going through the motions?\n\nPatient: Yeah, exactly. Like I mentioned when we first started talking, I barely care about any of those things right now. Doing them really does just feel like going through the motions. Dance class, the recipes, hiking, game night... none of it makes me feel like myself anymore. They just sit there like chores that I keep putting off.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling fatigued or like you don’t have any energy lately, even if you haven’t been doing much?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My energy is fine. I mean... I know I'm not doing much right now, but it's not because I'm physically tired or exhausted. I just don't have the interest in doing things, but my actual energy levels are pretty normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not worth much or have been blaming yourself for things more than usual lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have been. I just... I keep telling myself that I should have known better than to take that booking in the first place. I feel like I really failed the people who were helping me by not keeping them safe. Like you mentioned, dance class and trying new recipes... but also hiking, or even game night. They don't feel fun. They all just sit there now like chores that I keep putting off.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself or ending your life?\n\nPatient: No, no I haven't. I haven't really been keeping track of it that closely, to be honest. It definitely happens a lot lately, but... I don't know if I'd say it's most of the day or every day for the last few weeks. I just know it's been going on for a while now.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your lack of interest in doing things has been happening most of the day, nearly every day, rather than coming and going?\n\nPatient: Like I said, I haven't really been tracking it that closely. It definitely doesn't feel like it's all day, every day, though. It just feels like it's been happening a lot lately.
